# Commands · D · part 3

1,394 entries, design-iac-structure to discover. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **design-iac-structure**: Design the IaC estate: module decomposition, state isolation by blast radius, promotion model, and repo layout. ([mcorbett51090/RavenClaude](https://github.com/mcorbett51090/RavenClaude/blob/HEAD/plugins/terraform-iac/commands/design-iac-structure.md) · ★7)
- **design-identity-and-rbac**: Design passwordless identity and least-privilege RBAC for an Azure workload — managed identity / workload-identity-federation for runtime and CI/CD, narrowly-scoped built-in or custom roles at RG scope, PIM for elevation, no standing Owner. ([mcorbett51090/RavenClaude](https://github.com/mcorbett51090/RavenClaude/blob/HEAD/plugins/azure-cloud/commands/design-identity-and-rbac.md) · ★7)
- **design-implement**: Thin entry point for `/design-implement &lt;export-path|url&gt; [--route &lt;path&gt;] [--viewport WxH] [--from-screenshots] [--dry-run]` — parses and validates the argument shape only, then hands control to the `design-build` skill. No run logic lives here. ([atretyak1985/swarmery](https://github.com/atretyak1985/swarmery/blob/HEAD/plugins/design-pack/commands/design-implement.md) · ★7)
- **design-implement**: Implement UI from Claude Design handoff or manifest into Flutter design system. ([FreeOnur/BallUp](https://github.com/FreeOnur/BallUp/blob/HEAD/.cursor/commands/design-implement.md) · ★1)
- **design-import**: Scaffolds React components from a Claude Design handoff bundle and stops at files on disk: no stories, no tests, no pull request. Use when handed a claude.ai/design URL or a local bundle file; when that same scaffold should carry on through test generation, browser verification and an opened PR, run /ork:design-ship instead. ([yonatangross/orchestkit](https://github.com/yonatangross/orchestkit/blob/HEAD/plugins/ork/.cursor-plugin/commands/design-import.md) · ★278)
- **design-import**: Import an external design document (Markdown, JSON, or HTML from Confluence/Notion) and convert it to design_doc.json format for Plan Cascade integration. ([Taoidle/plan-cascade](https://github.com/Taoidle/plan-cascade/blob/HEAD/commands/design-import.md) · ★141)
- **design-improve**: Iterative design enhancement chain (audit → bolder → animate → colorize → overdrive). Phase 1 audit = ui-ux-designer critique (read-only) + frontend-specialist fixes; phases 2-5 spawn frontend-specialist foreground; ui-ux-designer review gate closes the chain. Skills: impeccable 4.x + ui-ux-pro-max + gpus-theme + grupo-us. Use --from=&lt;phase&gt; to resume. Ends with /verify quick. ([GrupoUS/otb-usa](https://github.com/GrupoUS/otb-usa/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/design-improve.md) · ★1)
- **design-infrastructure**: Use this command when designing cloud infrastructure for a new service or major redesign of existing infrastructure. - **diagnosing-bugs**: PHẢI dùng khi: đang stuck với bug khó reproduce, không biết debug từ đâu, đã fix nhiều lần mà bug vẫn quay lại, hoặc debug bừa theo kiểu 'thử cái này xem có hết không'. Cũng dùng khi: cần review quy trình debug hiện tại để chắc Phase 1 (feedback loop) đã sẵn sàng trước khi tiếp tục. KHÔNG dùng khi: đã có failing test và biết chính xác dòng code cần sửa (→ fix thẳng); đây là lỗi typo/config rõ ràng không cần debug loop (→ WF-FASTTRACK).
